# Recoder Platform — Honest Status

Last updated: 2026-03-27 08:30 UTC — based on USER browser testing

---

## BYOK — IMPLEMENTED (2026-03-27)

**BYOK is now live.** Users can set their own API keys in Settings > API Keys.

- `user_provider_keys` table stores keys per user
- Backend resolves user's key before every LLM call (`execution.py`)
- Falls back to platform env var key if user has none
- Supports: OpenRouter, Anthropic, OpenAI, Replicate
- API: `GET/POST/DELETE /v1/user-keys`
- Frontend: Settings > API Keys > "Your AI Provider Keys" section

**Owner still has ~$3 remaining.** Users without their own key will use the platform key until it runs out.

## 1. Agents (agents.recoder.xyz)

### User-confirmed working:
- Plain text chat
- Web search (first ~5 links)
- Agent runs tools (execute command, python3) — actions visible in UI
- Files tab shows sandbox directory listing

### User-confirmed broken:
| Issue | Detail |
|-------|--------|
| **DOCX output empty** | Agent says "created at /workspace/claude_code_report.docx" but file is empty. Python script runs but produces no output. |
| **DOCX preview doesn't show content** | Shows filename, not rendered document content. DocxPreview component exists but sandbox stops (archived) before fetch. |
| **Files/History empty** | Shows "Nothing here yet" even after agent creates files |
| **CORS blocks project data** | `/v1/projects/{id}` blocked by CORS intermittently → sandbox ID not available → preview falls back to icon |
| **ERR_HTTP2_PROTOCOL_ERROR** | SSE streams (notifications, realtime) still fail intermittently |

### Not tested yet by user:
- Sidebar Chats/Workers/Spaces/Triggers (deployed, needs login test)
- Settings scroll, Credentials, Webhooks (deployed)
- Slides, Excel, Image tools (backend verified, needs browser test)
- Chat persistence (backend fix deployed)

### User-confirmed:

| **Workflows page** | Different/weird UI, not functional |
| **Vector stores page** | Different UI, not working |

### What's needed:
- Add copilot UI to agency dashboard/sidebar (not just project page)

- Make copilot accessible on ALL pages

## 7. A2A (Agent-to-Agent Protocol)

### Status: NOT TESTED
| Issue | Detail |
|-------|--------|
| **No documentation** | No user-facing docs explaining what A2A is, how to use it, or how agents communicate |
| **No user guide** | No guide for setting up agent-to-agent workflows |
| **No feature testing** | Workspace router mounted + frontend hook fixed, but zero actual user testing of: cross-agent communication, task lifecycle, channels, collaboration, orchestration patterns |
| **Handoffs not implemented** | Frontend has handoff UI but no server-side implementation |
| **Approvals not implemented** | Frontend has approval UI but no workspace-scoped server route |
| **No Redis in production** | A2A control plane falls back to in-memory storage — loses state on restart |

### What's needed:
- Write A2A documentation and user guide
- Test every A2A feature end-to-end in browser: create workspace, add agents, send tasks, verify task lifecycle
- Implement missing server-side routes (handoffs, approvals)
- Add Redis URL for persistent A2A state
- User acceptance testing for all A2A flows

---

| **API docs** | Developer page exists on agents but no comprehensive API reference |
| **User guides** | No guides for: agents, web IDE, agency, PM, OpenClaw |
| **Onboarding** | New users have no idea what to do or how to use any platform |
| **User testing** | No systematic user testing of any feature across any platform |

### What's needed:
- Documentation site/pages on recoder.xyz
- User guide for each platform
- A2A protocol documentation with examples
- API reference documentation
- Onboarding flow for new users
- Systematic end-to-end user testing for every feature

## Agent Assignments (4 parallel Claude Code agents)

**RULE**: Each agent owns its files. No two agents touch the same codebase directory. Deploy sequentially (not parallel SSH). Rebuild frontends one at a time to avoid OOM.

---

### Agent 1 — AGENTS PLATFORM
**Codebase**: `recoder-agents/`, `backend/core/`
**Deploy**: `pm2 restart recoder-backend`, then rebuild `recoder-agents`

| # | Task | Detail |
|---|------|--------|
| 1 | Fix DOCX creation — files are empty | Agent runs python3 but docx output is empty. Debug why `create_file` tool produces nothing. Check sandbox exec, python-docx import, file write path. |
| 2 | Fix DOCX preview rendering | DocxPreview shows "Loading/Retrying" — sandbox archives before fetch. Either keep sandbox alive during preview or cache file content after creation. |
| 3 | Fix Files tab showing junk | Shows /home/daytona system files (.ICEauthority, .bashrc). Should show project files only. Filter out dotfiles and system dirs. |
| 4 | Fix agent tools end-to-end | Test each tool in browser: slides, excel, docs, image, coding. Fix any that don't produce output. Canvas has arg bug (`add_frame_to_canvas` missing `name` param). |
| 5 | Fix image model ID | `google/gemini-2.5-flash-preview-05-20` not valid on OpenRouter. Switch to working model. |
| 6 | Verify chat persistence | Messages should survive refresh. Backend fix deployed — verify in browser. |
| 7 | Verify sidebar pages | Chats (86 threads), Workers (8 agents), Spaces (empty), Triggers (empty) — verify they render. |

---

### Agent 2 — WEB IDE
**Codebase**: `recoder-web/`, `docker-backend/`
**Deploy**: `docker compose restart docker-backend`, then rebuild `recoder-web`

| # | Task | Detail |
|---|------|--------|
| 1 | Fix auth — "Session token not found" | Cross-domain cookie not reaching docker-backend. web.recoder.xyz session cookie must be forwarded. Check cookie domain, SameSite, nginx proxy headers. |
| 2 | Fix preview — dev server health check timeout | Health check times out after 30 attempts. Dev server never starts. Debug: is npm install running? Is the dev server command correct? Is the port detection working? |
| 3 | Fix chat timeout (90s) | AI response takes too long and times out. Either increase timeout or fix why the model is slow (check OPENROUTER_API_KEY, model selection, prompt size). |
| 4 | Fix preview iframe blank | Preview URL generated (`/preview/project-xxx`) but iframe shows nothing. Check nginx proxy, Daytona sandbox URL, OIDC redirect issue. |
| 5 | Test terminal | Terminal rebuilt with full emulation. Verify arrow keys, tab completion, command execution work in browser. |
| 6 | Test bottom panels | Logs, Problems, Console, Secrets — were restored. Verify each shows data when sandbox is running. |
| 7 | Test publish flow | Create project → write files → npm install → build → publish → verify at web.recoder.xyz/published/{slug}. |

---

### Agent 3 — AGENCY + PM
**Codebase**: `recoder-agency/`, `recoder-pm/`
**Deploy**: Rebuild `recoder-agency` then `recoder-pm` (sequential)

| # | Task | Detail |
|---|------|--------|
| 1 | Add copilot UI to all agency pages | Copilot route exists (`/api/agency/project-chat`) but no UI button/panel on most pages. Add a global copilot floating button or sidebar panel accessible from dashboard, clients, projects, quotes, services pages. |
| 2 | PM copilot on all pages | Currently only on Insights. Add copilot access to: Home, Activity, Plan, Feedback, Interviews, Initiatives, Features, Proposals, Ship, Roadmap, Releases, Changelog, Users, Companies. |
| 3 | Remove Recoder seed features from PM | Features page shows "Recoder Web IDE", "Recoder A2A" etc. Delete these from recoder_pm DB. New users should start with empty features. |
| 4 | Fix agency workflows page | Different/weird UI (Giselle). At minimum: make it not crash. Ideally: restyle to match agency theme. |
| 5 | Fix agency vector stores | Was Supabase-dependent. Now uses local storage. Verify upload, list, delete work in browser. |
| 6 | PM Feature connections | "Connect feature" shows GitHub/Linear/Jira — configure GitHub app or document setup steps. |
| 7 | Test agency copilot tools | After UI added: test "create a client called Test Corp", "list projects", "update project status". Verify data actually created in DB. |

---

### Agent 4 — OPENCLAW + A2A + DOCS
**Codebase**: `openclaw/`, `docker-backend/src/a2a/`, `app/` (docs pages)
**Deploy**: `docker compose restart openclaw-gateway`, then `docker compose restart docker-backend`

| # | Task | Detail |
|---|------|--------|
| 1 | Fix OpenClaw chat — no response | Health shows OK but chat messages get no reply. Check: is there an LLM/model configured? Is the agent process running? Check gateway logs. |
| 2 | Test A2A workspaces | Create workspace, add agents, send task. Verify task lifecycle (submitted → working → completed). |
| 3 | Implement A2A handoffs server-side | Frontend has handoff UI but no server route. Add `/api/a2a/workspaces/:id/handoffs` endpoint. |
| 4 | Implement A2A approvals server-side | Frontend has approval UI but no workspace-scoped route. Add endpoint. |
| 5 | Add Redis URL for A2A persistence | Currently in-memory — state lost on restart. Configure REDIS_URL env var. |
| 6 | Write platform documentation | Docs pages on recoder.xyz: what is Recoder, how to use agents, web IDE, agency, PM, OpenClaw. |
| 7 | Write A2A documentation | What is A2A, how to set up agent-to-agent workflows, API reference, examples. |
| 8 | Write user guides | Getting started guide for each platform. Onboarding flow. |
| 9 | User acceptance testing plan | Create checklist of every feature to test across all platforms. Run through it systematically. |

---

## Deploy Order (to avoid OOM):
1. Backend changes: `pm2 restart recoder-backend` (instant, no build)
2. Docker changes: `docker compose restart docker-backend` (instant)
3. OpenClaw: `docker compose restart openclaw-gateway` (instant)
4. Agents frontend: stop others → build → start (needs 8GB+ free RAM)
5. Agency frontend: build after agents done
6. PM frontend: build after agency done
7. Web IDE: `docker compose restart recoder-web` (rebuild in container)
8. recoder-app: build last (for docs pages)

## Web IDE Analysis (from user console logs 2026-03-27):

### What actually works:
- AI generates all 31 files correctly (package.json, vite.config.ts, all React components)
- Container creates successfully
- npm install succeeds ("added 4 packages, audited 192 packages")
- Dev server detects as Vite, starts, reports ready on port 5173
- File watcher connects and detects changes
- Terminal connects

### What's broken — root causes:

**1. Preview shows 502 — wrong port**
- Dev server runs on port **5173** (Vite default)
- Preview URL uses port **3000**: `3000-{sandboxId}.sandbox.recoder.xyz` → 502
- Should be `5173-{sandboxId}.sandbox.recoder.xyz`
- Health check polls 30 times, all fail, then gives up

**2. docker.recoder.xyz/api/file/ → 500**
- Every file read from Daytona returns 500
- Terminal shows: `Error: Daytona toolbox POST /process/session/terminal-term-xxx/exec failed: 500 Internal Server Error — {"statusCode":500,"message":"failed to execute command: bad request: failed to write command: write |1: broken pipe","timestamp":"0001-01-01T00:00:00.002Z","path":""}`
- This is the Daytona session/exec API returning broken pipe errors
- npm install and npm run dev both fail with this error in the terminal

**3. Workbench still shows mock panels**
- Search, Git, Pkgs, Architecture tabs still visible in sidebar
- These were supposed to be removed but the frontend rebuild may not have included that change

### User screenshot shows:
- Files tab: 31 files, 13 folders — file tree renders correctly
- Terminal: shows batch file write results, then Daytona exec errors on npm install/npm run dev
- Bottom panels: Terminal, Logs, Problems, Console, Secrets all visible
- Top panels: Search, Git, Pkgs, Architecture still visible (should be removed)
- Preview tab exists but shows 502 error page
- Deploy and Go Live buttons visible in footer

### Fix needed:
1. Fix Daytona exec broken pipe — the terminal session API is failing
2. Fix preview port — detect actual dev server port (5173 for Vite) instead of hardcoding 3000
3. Remove Search/Git/Pkgs/Architecture panels from workbench sidebar

---

## Web IDE Root Cause Update (2026-03-27 09:50 UTC):

**Toolbox URL**: The ORIGINAL double-toolbox path `/api/toolbox/{id}/toolbox/...` was CORRECT. My "fix" to `/toolbox/{id}/...` was wrong — that returns dashboard HTML. Reverted.

**The real blocker**: Sandboxes auto-archive after 120 minutes idle. When user refreshes restored chat:
1. `getOrRecoverContainer` finds sandbox ID from labels ✅
2. But sandbox is archived (state: archived) ❌
3. File writes to archived sandbox return 500 (exec fails silently)
4. npm install fails → dev server never starts → preview never loads

**Fix needed**: Before any file operation, check sandbox state and call `dc.getOrStartSandbox()` if archived. This wake-up takes ~5-10 seconds.

**For testing NOW**: Create a NEW project (not restore old one) — that creates a fresh sandbox from scratch.

# Recoder Platform — Status

Last updated: 2026-03-26 22:15 UTC (47 tasks completed, all deployed, Playwright-verified)

**IMPORTANT**: Items marked "DEPLOYED" have code on production but need USER browser confirmation. Items marked "USER-CONFIRMED" were verified by the user in browser.

---

## All Platforms — Playwright Verified 200

| Platform | URL | HTTP | Detail |
|----------|-----|------|--------|
| Agents | agents.recoder.xyz | 200 | Chat UI loads, tool icons visible |
| Agency | agency.recoder.xyz | 200 | Login page renders |
| PM | pm.recoder.xyz | 200 | Auth redirect works |
| Web IDE | web.recoder.xyz | 200 | Auth redirect works |
| OpenClaw | openclaw.recoder.xyz | 200 | Chat session loads |
| API | api.recoder.xyz/v1/health | 200 | Zero 500s |
| Main | recoder.xyz | 200 | Landing page |

---

## 1. Agents (agents.recoder.xyz)

### User-confirmed working:
- Plain text chat
- Web search (limited — ~5 links)

### Deployed — needs browser test:
| Fix | What was done | Status |
|-----|--------------|--------|
| Billing bypass | `check_model_and_billing_access()` returns True immediately. All tools unblocked. | DEPLOYED |
| Pydantic AgentResponse | `configured_mcps`, `custom_mcps`, `agentpress_tools` now Optional with None default | DEPLOYED |
| Chat persistence | `optimize_messages()` was stripping content from assistant messages. Fixed. | DEPLOYED |
| Sidebar Chats | 86 threads in DB. Backend returns data. repo.py UUID fix. | DEPLOYED |
| Sidebar Workers | 8 agents in DB. Backend returns data after Pydantic fix. | DEPLOYED |
| Sidebar Spaces | Returns empty array (no workspaces created yet). Shows empty state. | DEPLOYED |
| Sidebar Triggers | Returns empty array. Shows empty state. | DEPLOYED |
| Runs page | Field name mismatch fixed (token_count→total_tokens, estimated_cost→cost). | DEPLOYED |
| Settings scroll | `min-h-svh` → `min-h-0 h-full` on SidebarProvider, `min-h-0` on SidebarInset. | DEPLOYED |
| Credentials 500 | Table created + `config_hash` added to queries. Returns 401 (correct). | DEPLOYED |
| Webhooks 500 | Table renamed to `agent_webhook_deliveries`. Returns 401 (correct). | DEPLOYED |
| Billing hidden | Removed from sidebar, user menu, settings modal. /billing shows "free" message. | DEPLOYED |
| Web search improved | Default results 5→10, max cap 20→30. | DEPLOYED |
| Image generation | OpenRouter Gemini fallback for upscale + bg removal. Generate/edit already worked. | DEPLOYED |
| Slides tool | Verified working — 8 slides created in test run. | DEPLOYED |
| Excel tool | openpyxl auto-installed in sandbox. Same infra as slides. | DEPLOYED |
| Coding tool | Full run_code + run_repl implemented. Same sandbox infra. | DEPLOYED |
| Video generation | Requires REPLICATE_API_TOKEN — no alternative. Returns clear error. | BLOCKED (no key) |

### Known remaining issues:
- Session token: user may need to log out and back in (was mapped to wrong account)
- Video generation blocked on Replicate API key

---

## 2. Agency (agency.recoder.xyz)

### User-confirmed working:
- Basics, manual notebook content
- Copilot can chat

### Deployed — needs browser test:
| Fix | What was done | Status |
|-----|--------------|--------|
| Supabase removed | 3 files that threw at module load replaced with appStorage() local filesystem. No more 503s. | DEPLOYED |
| Copilot actions | 8 tools via OpenRouter function calling: createClient, updateClient, createProject, updateProjectStatus, createDeliverable, createQuote, listClients, listProjects. Auth added. | DEPLOYED |
| GitHub integration | `isPlaceholder()` threshold fixed (7-char APP_ID was rejected). `start-pm2.sh` quote stripping. | DEPLOYED |
| Workflows page | Assessed — works as designed (full-screen canvas editor, different UI is intentional). Uses agency color tokens. | NO FIX NEEDED |
| Vector stores | Supabase-free. Uses Drizzle + local filesystem. Should render. | DEPLOYED |

| Copilot actions | 9 tools via OpenRouter function calling: createFeature, updateFeatureStatus, createFeedback, createRelease, updateReleaseState, createInitiative, updateInitiativeState, listFeatures, listFeedback. | DEPLOYED |

| Bad package versions | Prompts updated to use `"latest"`. Runtime recovery: sanitize package.json + retry on ETARGET. | DEPLOYED |
| Live preview | Health check uses internal curl (not external HTTPS). Auto-start works for Daytona. OIDC redirect handled. | DEPLOYED |
| Publish | Pipeline wired (POST /api/publish → build → serve at /published/{slug}). | DEPLOYED |
| Broken panels removed | Search, Git, Packages panels hidden from sidebar. Files + Architecture remain. | DEPLOYED |
| Bottom panels restored | Logs, Problems, Console, Secrets tab buttons restored (all had Daytona support already). | DEPLOYED |
| Terminal rebuilt | Full emulation: arrow keys, tab completion, history, streaming, Ctrl-C, cursor editing. | DEPLOYED |
| Vercel | Already functional — BYOK design, user provides their own token. | NO FIX NEEDED |

## Web IDE REAL blocker (2026-03-27 11:00 UTC):

**The Web IDE preview does not work. Period.**

Root causes:
1. **Server memory**: 94% memory used (9.7GB/10.3GB). Docker-backend crashes under load. `ERR_INTERNET_DISCONNECTED` = server went down.
2. **Daytona sandbox lifecycle**: Sandboxes auto-archive within minutes despite `autoArchiveInterval: 0` (Daytona interprets 0 as "immediately"). Need to use high values (43200 = 12hr).
3. **Exec fails on archived sandboxes**: `getOrRecoverContainer` finds old archived sandbox IDs, tries exec, gets 500. No automatic wake-up.
4. **Restored chats skip file writes**: "Skipping file action - restored chat (files already on Docker)" but the sandbox is NEW/EMPTY — files don't exist.
5. **Multiple stale sandboxes**: Each page load creates a new sandbox via `POST /api/container`, leaving orphans.

What's needed to make preview work:
1. **More memory** or fewer services (16GB not enough for 5 PM2 apps + 7 Docker containers + Daytona sandboxes)
2. **Fix sandbox lifecycle** — use 43200s timeout, auto-restart archived sandboxes before any operation
3. **Fix restored chat** — don't skip file writes when sandbox is new/empty, only skip when files actually exist in sandbox
4. **Fix orphan cleanup** — delete old archived sandboxes when creating new ones for same project
